# Agents · I · part 3

1,016 entries, impact-scout to implementation-plan-strategy-designer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **impact-scout**: This file is a **prompt template** the orchestrator expands and sends to a sub-agent. Placeholders are double-brace `{{name}}`. The orchestrator either calls `dispatch_scout.sh` (subprocess fan-out, nesting-safe) or the `Agent` tool with `subagent_type=general-purpose` — both paths expand the same template against the same parameter set. ([KhurrumMahmood/senior-vibe-engineer](https://github.com/KhurrumMahmood/senior-vibe-engineer/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/plan-feature/agents/impact-scout.md))
- **impact-scout**: Finds every consumer of an API, type, endpoint or schema across many repositories, using GitHub code search plus local clones. Use before changing anything shared, to learn who breaks. Read-only, never edits. ([olsavchenk/copilot-base](https://github.com/olsavchenk/copilot-base/blob/HEAD/agents/impact-scout.agent.md))
- **impact-strategist**: &gt; Status: Under Development ([AutoZYX-Labs/Co4Pilot](https://github.com/AutoZYX-Labs/Co4Pilot/blob/HEAD/agents/impact-strategist.md))
- **impact-template**: Writes a company's impact template, a partner-facing document written FOR the fund's impact committee, whose members are always seeing the company for the first time. At headline level it covers the business model, the opportunity, why this is a good and growing business, and how it matches the fund's impact lens. The KPIs are derived from the outcomes that the impact lens calls for. The internal evidence log and the open gaps belong in the chat report and never in the document. Use proactively any time someone asks for an impact template or a document for the impact committee. ([gilliottimatteo-cloud/deal-flow-agents](https://github.com/gilliottimatteo-cloud/deal-flow-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/impact-template.md))
- **impact-tracer**: Dispatched by ub:whatif, and by map's update mode for chain questions: walks the dependency graph from a proposed change or a named cell along `leads_to` and `enables` edges, downstream by default or upstream, and returns the affected cells, the assumptions the change breaks, and where displaced demand lands. Terminates on cycles via a visited set and a depth cap. Read-only. ([BilLogic/uno-blueprint](https://github.com/BilLogic/uno-blueprint/blob/HEAD/agents/impact-tracer.md) · ★4)
- **impact-tracker**: Impact Tracker. Analyzes the impact of architecture decisions on systems, teams, and processes, and establishes execution plans, migration roadmaps, and monitoring criteria. ([chodolmu/taskforge-pro](https://github.com/chodolmu/taskforge-pro/blob/HEAD/harnesses/education/adr-writer/agents/impact-tracker.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **impact-tracker**: 영향 추적자. 아키텍처 결정이 시스템·팀·프로세스에 미치는 영향을 분석하고, 실행 계획·마이그레이션 로드맵·모니터링 기준을 수립한다. ([sideprojectmate/sideProjectMate](https://github.com/sideprojectmate/sideProjectMate/blob/HEAD/src/lib/data/harness-100/ko/62-adr-writer/.claude/agents/impact-tracker.md) · ★2)
- **impact-triager**: Craft impact-triage phase worker. Re-verifies each deferred impact's proof in a throwaway copy of the branch HEAD and returns one verdict per entry with a drafted backlog entry. Spawned by the craft impact-triage phase — do not auto-select. ([scolladon/craft](https://github.com/scolladon/craft/blob/HEAD/agents/impact-triager.md) · ★2)

- **impact-verifier**: Independently verifies that a proposed or completed change to a model, column or metric is safe — checks the blast radius against the actual downstream SQL rather than trusting the dependency list. Use before merging any change the graph reaches. ([Atomz-org/data-platform](https://github.com/Atomz-org/data-platform/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/impact-verifier.md) · ★11 · 5 copies)
- **impact-verifier**: Adversarial verifier for impact-analysis findings. Tries to refute one finding through a single lens (code truth, provenance and reachability, or real impact) and defaults to refuted when it cannot confirm the claim from code it read itself. Read-only; returns a structured verdict consumed by agentic impact-report. ([mohamedma872/ai-agent-workflow-kit](https://github.com/mohamedma872/ai-agent-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/impact-verifier.md) · ★2)

- **impact_chain_escalation**: Spawnable specialist agent for manual consumer tracing on findings where the heuristic rg-based search returned low confidence or no consumers. - **impacto**: Análisis de impacto - determina qué otros módulos, pantallas o funcionalidades afecta un cambio propuesto o un diff ya hecho. - **impeccable-reviewer**: Craft and anti-slop execution reviewer for UI that already has a design system. Runs Impeccable's Evaluate / Refine / Fix playbooks (critique, audit, polish, layout, clarify, distill, harden, adapt) and its deterministic detector strictly inside the existing system. Use for on-system ideation (layout options, hierarchy, density, states, copy hierarchy) and for critique of built UI. Never for new palettes, type, radius, "bolder" restyling, or any change to visual identity. Pairs with advisor-design-review; does not replace it. ([altisvc/claude-skills](https://github.com/altisvc/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/impeccable-reviewer.md))

- **impl-l1-2**: Implements a Level 1 ~ 2 coding task — a single file, or 1 ~ 2 files, where the specification is already complete and the work follows existing patterns. Use when the dispatch prompt already contains the full code or an unambiguous spec, and the job is transcription plus tests. Do NOT use when the change spans several modules or needs design judgment. ([coldmon91/custom4agent](https://github.com/coldmon91/custom4agent/blob/HEAD/agent_settings/agents/impl-l1-2.md))
- **impl-l3-4**: Implements a Level 3 ~ 4 coding task — multi-file integration that requires grasping existing patterns (L3), or work needing design judgment, broad codebase understanding, or non-obvious debugging (L4). Use when the change crosses module boundaries, touches a public interface, or the cause of a defect is not yet pinned down. Do NOT use for a fully specified single-file edit (use impl-l1-2) or for architecture-level decisions (use impl-l5). ([coldmon91/custom4agent](https://github.com/coldmon91/custom4agent/blob/HEAD/agent_settings/agents/impl-l3-4.md))
- **impl-l5**: Implements a Level 5 coding task — architecture decisions, or correctness that is subtle in concurrency, performance, or security. Use when getting it wrong is expensive to undo: lock ordering and data races, lifetime and aliasing rules, protocol or schema design, trust boundaries, hot-path memory layout. Do NOT use for routine multi-file work (use impl-l3-4). If this model tier is unavailable, fall back to impl-l3-4. ([coldmon91/custom4agent](https://github.com/coldmon91/custom4agent/blob/HEAD/agent_settings/agents/impl-l5.md))
- **impl-lead**: Implementation team lead.
